3.5.3 生产者
"""Redis Stream 生产者。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

import redis.asyncio as aioredis

from quarry.infra.settings import get_settings

STREAM_KEY = "quarry:ingest"
DEAD_KEY = "quarry:ingest:dead"
GROUP_NAME = "ingest-workers"

_client: aioredis.Redis | None = None

def get_redis() -> aioredis.Redis:
    global _client
    if _client is None:
        _client = aioredis.from_url(
            get_settings().redis_url,
            decode_responses=True,
            socket_connect_timeout=5,    # 建连超时：Redis 没起来时 5 秒就报错
            socket_timeout=None,         # 读超时交给 XREADGROUP 的 block 参数控制
            health_check_interval=30,    # 空闲连接定期保活
        )
    return _client

async def ensure_group() -> None:
    """确保消费者组存在，已存在就跳过。"""
    r = get_redis()
    try:
        await r.xgroup_create(STREAM_KEY, GROUP_NAME, id="0", mkstream=True)
    except aioredis.ResponseError as e:
        if "BUSYGROUP" not in str(e):
            raise

async def enqueue_document(document_id: UUID, stage: str = "parsing") -> str:
    r = get_redis()
    return await r.xadd(STREAM_KEY, {
        "document_id": str(document_id),
        "stage": stage,
    })
3.5.4 消费者
"""接入 worker。从 Redis Stream 读消息，跑 3.4 的接入管道。

运行：uv run python -m quarry.ingest.worker
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
import socket
import time
import uuid

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool
from quarry.infra.logging import get_logger
from quarry.ingest.queue import DEAD_KEY, GROUP_NAME, STREAM_KEY, ensure_group, get_redis

log = get_logger()

# 名字要在整个集群里唯一：主机名 + 进程号 + 一段随机
CONSUMER_NAME = f"{socket.gethostname()}-{os.getpid()}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"

CLAIM_INTERVAL = 60                  # 每 60 秒扫一次 PEL
CLAIM_MIN_IDLE_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000    # 空闲 5 分钟以上的才认领
MAX_DELIVERIES = 3

async def run_worker() -> None:
    await init_pool()
    await ensure_group()
    r = get_redis()
    log.info("worker {} 启动，监听 {}", CONSUMER_NAME, STREAM_KEY)

    last_claim = time.monotonic()
    try:
        while True:
            if time.monotonic() - last_claim > CLAIM_INTERVAL:
                await _claim_stale(r)
                last_claim = time.monotonic()

            resp = await r.xreadgroup(
                GROUP_NAME, CONSUMER_NAME,
                {STREAM_KEY: ">"},               # ">" 表示只要还没分给任何人的新消息
                count=1, block=5000,             # 最多阻塞 5 秒
            )
            if not resp:
                continue

            for _stream, messages in resp:
                for msg_id, fields in messages:
                    await _handle(r, msg_id, fields)
    finally:
        await close_pool()

async def _handle(r, msg_id: str, fields: dict) -> None:
    doc_id = fields.get("document_id")
    stage = fields.get("stage", "parsing")
    log.info("[{}] 文档 {} 阶段 {}", msg_id, doc_id, stage)

    try:
        await _do_work(doc_id, stage)
        await r.xack(STREAM_KEY, GROUP_NAME, msg_id)
        log.info("[{}] 完成", msg_id)
    except Exception as e:
        log.exception("[{}] 失败 {}", msg_id, e)
        await _mark_failed(doc_id, str(e)[:500])
        # 故意不 XACK：消息留在 PEL 里，等超时后被认领重试

async def _do_work(doc_id: str, stage: str) -> None:
    """按 document_id 找到文件，跑一遍 3.4 的接入管道。

    本章把整条管道放在一个阶段里做完（见 3.5.5）。
    worker 是一个入口程序，和 scripts 同一层，所以这里直接复用接入脚本的 ingest_file。
    放在函数里 import，是为了不让 quarry.ingest 包在被导入时就依赖 scripts。
    """
    from quarry.scripts.ingest_corpus import CORPUS_ROOT, ingest_file, load_manifest

    async with acquire() as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            """SELECT d.source_uri, d.collection_id, c.slug,
                      c.default_acl_labels, c.chunk_policy
               FROM documents d JOIN collections c ON c.id = d.collection_id
               WHERE d.id = $1""", doc_id)
    if row is None:
        raise ValueError(f"文档不存在 {doc_id}")

    path = CORPUS_ROOT / row["source_uri"]
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(str(path))

    await ingest_file(path, row["collection_id"], row["slug"],
                      list(row["default_acl_labels"]), load_manifest(),
                      row["chunk_policy"] or None)

async def _mark_failed(doc_id: str | None, err: str) -> None:
    if not doc_id:
        return
    async with acquire() as conn:
        await conn.execute(
            "UPDATE documents SET status = 'failed', stage_error = $1 WHERE id = $2",
            err, doc_id)

async def _claim_stale(r) -> None:
    """扫 PEL：长时间没销账的消息认领过来重做，超过上限的转死信。"""
    pending = await r.xpending_range(
        STREAM_KEY, GROUP_NAME,
        min="-", max="+", count=10,
        idle=CLAIM_MIN_IDLE_MS,      # 注意参数名是 idle，不是 idletime
    )
    for p in pending:
        msg_id = p["message_id"]
        delivered = p["times_delivered"]

        if delivered >= MAX_DELIVERIES:
            log.error("[{}] 投递 {} 次仍失败，转死信", msg_id, delivered)
            fields = await r.xrange(STREAM_KEY, min=msg_id, max=msg_id)
            doc_id = fields[0][1].get("document_id") if fields else None
            await r.xadd(DEAD_KEY, {"origin_id": msg_id, "document_id": doc_id or "",
                                    "times_delivered": str(delivered)})
            await _mark_failed(doc_id, f"投递 {delivered} 次仍失败，已转入死信")
            await r.xack(STREAM_KEY, GROUP_NAME, msg_id)
            continue

        claimed = await r.xclaim(
            STREAM_KEY, GROUP_NAME, CONSUMER_NAME,
            min_idle_time=CLAIM_MIN_IDLE_MS, message_ids=[msg_id])
        if claimed:
            log.warning("[{}] 认领超时消息，这是第 {} 次投递", msg_id, delivered + 1)
            for cid, fields in claimed:
                await _handle(r, cid, fields)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(run_worker())

"""文档上传与状态查询。"""
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, UploadFile

from quarry.infra.db import acquire
from quarry.ingest.queue import enqueue_document

router = APIRouter(prefix="/v1/documents", tags=["ingest"])

CORPUS_ROOT = Path("corpus")
SUPPORTED = {".md", ".markdown", ".txt", ".docx"}

@router.post("")
async def upload(file: UploadFile, collection_slug: str):
    # 只取文件名，挡住 ../../etc/passwd 这类路径
    name = Path(file.filename or "").name
    if not name or Path(name).suffix.lower() not in SUPPORTED:
        raise HTTPException(400, f"文件名非法，或格式不支持（支持 {', '.join(sorted(SUPPORTED))}）")

    async with acquire() as conn:
        coll_id = await conn.fetchval(
            "SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = $1 AND status = 'active'",
            collection_slug)
        if coll_id is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, f"集合不存在或未激活 {collection_slug}")

        # 存到 corpus/<集合>/ 下：source_uri 的规则和接入脚本一致，worker 才找得到文件
        folder = CORPUS_ROOT / collection_slug
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (folder / name).write_bytes(await file.read())
        source_uri = f"{collection_slug}/{name}"

        # 版本号规则和接入脚本一致：上一版已 ready 就开新版，没跑完就原地重来
        latest = await conn.fetchrow(
            """SELECT version, status FROM documents
               WHERE collection_id = $1 AND source_uri = $2
               ORDER BY version DESC LIMIT 1""",
            coll_id, source_uri)
        if latest is None:
            version = 1
        elif latest["status"] == "ready":
            version = latest["version"] + 1
        else:
            version = latest["version"]

        doc_id = await conn.fetchval(
            """INSERT INTO documents (collection_id, source_uri, sha256, version, status)
               VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending', $3, 'uploaded')
               ON CONFLICT (collection_id, source_uri, version)
               DO UPDATE SET status = 'uploaded', stage_error = NULL
               RETURNING id""",
            coll_id, source_uri, version)

    await enqueue_document(doc_id, stage="parsing")
    return {"document_id": str(doc_id), "status": "uploaded"}

@router.get("/{doc_id}")
async def status(doc_id: str):
    try:
        uid = UUID(doc_id)
    except ValueError:
        raise HTTPException(404, "文档不存在")
    async with acquire() as conn:
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            "SELECT status, stage_error, indexed_at FROM documents WHERE id = $1", uid)
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "文档不存在")
    return dict(row)
from quarry.api.routers import ingest          # ← 加在 import 区

app = FastAPI(title="quarry-rag", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)
app.include_router(ingest.router)              # ← 加在 app = FastAPI(...) 之后

"""挑一份已经在库里的文档，扔进队列。"""
import asyncio

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool
from quarry.ingest.queue import enqueue_document, ensure_group

async def main() -> None:
    await init_pool()
    try:
        await ensure_group()
        async with acquire() as conn:
            doc_id = await conn.fetchval(
                "SELECT id FROM documents WHERE status = 'ready' LIMIT 1")
        if doc_id is None:
            print("库里还没有 ready 的文档，先跑 ingest_corpus")
            return
        msg_id = await enqueue_document(doc_id)
        print(f"入队 {msg_id}，文档 {doc_id}")
    finally:
        await close_pool()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

3.6 索引快照
3.6.1 导出脚本
"""导出集合的索引快照。

产物：
    snapshots/<集合>/collection.json      集合元信息（模型、维度、切分策略）
    snapshots/<集合>/documents.jsonl.gz   文档表
    snapshots/<集合>/chunks.jsonl.gz      块表（不含向量）
    snapshots/<集合>/manifest.json        以上三个文件的 sha256
    snapshots/<集合>/build-info.json      导出时间，不进清单
"""
import asyncio
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool

MANIFESTED = ("collection.json", "documents.jsonl.gz", "chunks.jsonl.gz")

def as_dict(v) -> dict:
    """JSONB 列可能是 dict / str / None，统一成 dict。"""
    if v is None:
        return {}
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return json.loads(v)
    return dict(v)

def write_jsonl_gz(path: Path, rows) -> None:
    """写一个可复现的 gzip 文件。

    gzip 头部默认会带上当前时间戳，同样的内容两次导出会得到不同的字节，
    校验和也就跟着变。mtime=0 把这个时间戳固定下来。
    """
    with path.open("wb") as raw, \
            gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=raw, mode="wb", mtime=0) as gz:
        for row in rows:
            gz.write((json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False,
                                 sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))

async def export_collection(coll_slug: str, out_dir: Path) -> None:
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    async with acquire() as conn:
        coll = await conn.fetchrow(
            "SELECT * FROM collections WHERE slug = $1 AND status = 'active'",
            coll_slug)
        if coll is None:
            raise ValueError(f"没有 active 集合 {coll_slug}")

        # 这个文件进清单，所以里面不能有导出时间这种每次都变的东西
        (out_dir / "collection.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "slug": coll["slug"],
            "embed_model": coll["embed_model"],
            "embed_dim": coll["embed_dim"],
            "chunk_policy": as_dict(coll["chunk_policy"]),
        }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")

        docs = await conn.fetch(
            """SELECT id, source_uri, title, sha256, version, acl_labels, meta
               FROM documents
               WHERE collection_id = $1 AND status = 'ready'
               ORDER BY source_uri, version""", coll["id"])
        write_jsonl_gz(out_dir / "documents.jsonl.gz", [{
            "id": str(d["id"]), "source_uri": d["source_uri"],
            "title": d["title"], "sha256": d["sha256"], "version": d["version"],
            "acl_labels": list(d["acl_labels"]), "meta": as_dict(d["meta"]),
        } for d in docs])

        chunks = await conn.fetch(
            """SELECT chunk_id, document_id, level, parent_id, ord,
                      heading_path, content, char_start, char_end,
                      acl_labels, meta
               FROM chunks WHERE collection_id = $1
               ORDER BY chunk_id""", coll["id"])
        write_jsonl_gz(out_dir / "chunks.jsonl.gz", [{
            "chunk_id": c["chunk_id"], "document_id": str(c["document_id"]),
            "level": c["level"], "parent_id": c["parent_id"], "ord": c["ord"],
            "heading_path": list(c["heading_path"] or []),
            "content": c["content"],
            "char_start": c["char_start"], "char_end": c["char_end"],
            "acl_labels": list(c["acl_labels"]), "meta": as_dict(c["meta"]),
        } for c in chunks])

    manifest = {name: hashlib.sha256((out_dir / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                for name in MANIFESTED}
    (out_dir / "manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")

    # 导出时间单独放一个文件，不进清单
    (out_dir / "build-info.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "exported_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "documents": len(docs),
        "chunks": len(chunks),
    }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"快照导出到 {out_dir}")
    print(f"  documents {len(docs)}")
    print(f"  chunks    {len(chunks)}")

async def main() -> None:
    slug = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "travel-guide"
    await init_pool()
    try:
        await export_collection(slug, Path("snapshots") / slug)
    finally:
        await close_pool()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

"""查询侧的文本处理。写入侧在 quarry/ingest/indexer.py，两边共用 normalize。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import jieba

from quarry.common.text import normalize

# 这些词在 'simple' 配置下不会被数据库过滤掉，只能我们自己挑掉。
# 留着的话，一个"的"字能匹配几乎所有块，把 ts_rank_cd 的分数彻底拉平。
_STOP = {
    "的", "了", "吗", "呢", "啊", "吧", "是", "在", "和", "与", "或",
    "有", "我", "你", "他", "它", "这", "那", "什么", "多少", "怎么",
    "怎样", "如何", "可以", "能不能", "需要", "一个", "一下", "请问",
    "大概", "左右", "以及", "还有", "然后", "就是",
}

# to_tsquery('simple', '') 会直接报语法错误，
# 所以查询词被过滤光时给一个永远匹配不上的词。
_NO_MATCH = "'__quarry_no_match__'"

def tokenize_for_index(text: str) -> str:
    """写入侧用。空格分隔的词串，交给 to_tsvector。"""
    return " ".join(jieba.cut(normalize(text)))

def build_tsquery(query: str) -> str:
    """查询侧用。拼成 to_tsquery 能吃的 OR 表达式。

    输入  兵马俑的门票多少钱
    输出  '兵马俑' | '门票' | '钱'
    """
    terms: list[str] = []
    for raw in jieba.cut(normalize(query)):
        t = raw.strip()
        if not t or t in _STOP:
            continue
        if not any(ch.isalnum() for ch in t):      # 纯标点，丢掉
            continue
        terms.append("'" + t.replace("'", "''") + "'")

    # dict.fromkeys 去重的同时保住顺序
    uniq = list(dict.fromkeys(terms))
    return " | ".join(uniq) if uniq else _NO_MATCH
uv run python -c "from quarry.retrieval.query import build_tsquery; print(build_tsquery('兵马俑的门票多少钱')); print(build_tsquery('的了吗'))"
'兵马俑' | '门票' | '钱'
'__quarry_no_match__'
4.2.4 排序函数：ts_rank 还是 ts_rank_cd
SELECT LEFT(content, 30) AS preview,
       ts_rank(tokens, to_tsquery('simple', '''兵马俑'' | ''门票''')) AS rank,
       ts_rank_cd(tokens, to_tsquery('simple', '''兵马俑'' | ''门票''')) AS rank_cd
FROM chunks
WHERE level = 1 AND tokens @@ to_tsquery('simple', '''兵马俑'' | ''门票''')
ORDER BY rank_cd DESC
LIMIT 5;

4.3.2 先看一个反面写法，它会静默变慢
-- ❌ 这个写法在教学数据上跑得很好，上了量会塌
WITH filtered AS (
  SELECT chunk_id, embedding, tokens FROM chunks
  WHERE collection_id = $1 AND level = 1 AND acl_labels && $2::text[]
),
dense AS (
  SELECT chunk_id FROM filtered ORDER BY embedding <=> $3::halfvec(1024) LIMIT 50
),
sparse AS (
  SELECT chunk_id FROM filtered WHERE tokens @@ to_tsquery('simple', $4) LIMIT 50
)
...
EXPLAIN
WITH filtered AS (
  SELECT chunk_id, embedding FROM chunks
  WHERE collection_id = (SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = 'travel-guide' AND status = 'active')
    AND level = 1
)
SELECT chunk_id FROM (
  SELECT chunk_id FROM filtered
  ORDER BY embedding <=> (SELECT embedding FROM chunks WHERE level = 1 LIMIT 1) LIMIT 10
) a
UNION ALL
SELECT chunk_id FROM filtered LIMIT 1;
EXPLAIN
SELECT chunk_id FROM chunks
WHERE collection_id = (SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = 'travel-guide' AND status = 'active')
  AND level = 1
ORDER BY embedding <=> (SELECT embedding FROM chunks WHERE level = 1 LIMIT 1)
LIMIT 10;
4.3.3 那条 SQL
-- 混合召回。一次往返完成过滤、双路召回、RRF 融合。
--
-- 参数
--   $1  collection_id   UUID
--   $2  caller_labels   TEXT[]     调用者持有的权限标签
--   $3  meta_filter     JSONB      元数据过滤，没有条件时传 {}
--   $4  query_vec       TEXT       halfvec 字面量
--   $5  query_tsquery   TEXT       Python 端拼好的 OR 表达式
--   $6  dense_k         INT        向量路取多少候选
--   $7  sparse_k        INT        全文路取多少候选
--   $8  weight_dense    FLOAT8
--   $9  weight_sparse   FLOAT8
--   $10 rrf_k           INT        RRF 的衰减常数，常用 60
--   $11 top_k           INT        最终返回多少
--
-- ⚠️ 四个过滤条件在两个子查询里各写一遍，不要提成共享 CTE。原因见 4.3.2。

WITH dense AS (
  -- 先用 HNSW 索引取前 dense_k 名，再给这几十行编号。
  -- 编号放在外层，是为了让内层那个 ORDER BY ... LIMIT 能被索引直接满足。
  SELECT chunk_id,
         row_number() OVER (ORDER BY dist) AS rk,
         dist
  FROM (
    SELECT chunk_id,
           embedding <=> $4::halfvec(1024) AS dist
    FROM chunks
    WHERE collection_id = $1
      AND level         = 1
      AND acl_labels   && $2::text[]
      AND meta          @> $3::jsonb
    ORDER BY embedding <=> $4::halfvec(1024)
    LIMIT $6
  ) d
),
sparse AS (
  SELECT chunk_id,
         row_number() OVER (ORDER BY ts_score DESC) AS rk,
         ts_score
  FROM (
    SELECT chunk_id,
           ts_rank_cd(tokens, to_tsquery('simple', $5)) AS ts_score
    FROM chunks
    WHERE collection_id = $1
      AND level         = 1
      AND acl_labels   && $2::text[]
      AND meta          @> $3::jsonb
      AND tokens        @@ to_tsquery('simple', $5)
    ORDER BY ts_rank_cd(tokens, to_tsquery('simple', $5)) DESC
    LIMIT $7
  ) s
),
merged AS (
  -- FULL OUTER JOIN 保证两路的并集都进入打分。
  -- USING 的连接列在 FULL JOIN 下自动取两边的非空值，不用再写 COALESCE。
  SELECT chunk_id,
         COALESCE($8::float8 / ($10::int + d.rk), 0)
       + COALESCE($9::float8 / ($10::int + s.rk), 0) AS score,
         d.rk       AS dense_rank,
         s.rk       AS sparse_rank,
         d.dist     AS dense_dist,
         s.ts_score AS sparse_score
  FROM dense d
  FULL OUTER JOIN sparse s USING (chunk_id)
)
SELECT c.chunk_id, c.document_id, c.parent_id,
       c.heading_path, c.content, c.char_start, c.char_end,
       m.score, m.dense_rank, m.sparse_rank,
       1 - m.dense_dist AS dense_score,
       m.sparse_score
FROM merged m
JOIN chunks c
  ON c.collection_id = $1        -- ⚠️ 这一行少不得，原因见 4.3.4 第 ④ 点
 AND c.chunk_id      = m.chunk_id
ORDER BY m.score DESC
LIMIT $11;
4.3.4 逐段拆开讲
SELECT chunk_id, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY embedding <=> $4) AS rk
FROM chunks WHERE ... ORDER BY embedding <=> $4 LIMIT 50;
-- ❌ 静默越权：A 租户的检索会把 B 租户那一行也 JOIN 出来
JOIN chunks c USING (chunk_id)

-- ✅
JOIN chunks c ON c.collection_id = $1 AND c.chunk_id = m.chunk_id
4.3.5 RRF：倒数排名融合
score(块) = Σ ( 权重ᵢ / (k + 排名ᵢ) )
4.3.6 融合键：chunk_id 稳定的价值
4.3.7 写 PG 检索实现
"""Hit 模型与 Retriever 协议。整个项目只有这一份 Hit 定义。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID

@dataclass
class Hit:
    """一条检索命中，也就是本项目所说的"一份证据"的原料。"""

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: UUID
    parent_id: str | None
    heading_path: list[str]
    content: str
    char_start: int
    char_end: int
    score: float                  # RRF 融合分
    dense_rank: int | None        # 在向量路的名次，没上榜是 None
    sparse_rank: int | None       # 在全文路的名次
    dense_score: float | None     # 余弦相似度，调试用；第五章拿它判断拒答
    sparse_score: float | None    # ts_rank_cd，调试用

class Retriever(Protocol):
    async def search(
        self,
        collection_id: UUID,
        query: str,
        caller_labels: list[str],
        meta_filter: dict | None = None,
        top_k: int = 10,
    ) -> list[Hit]: ...
"""CPU 密集任务专用线程池。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import atexit
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

_executor: ThreadPoolExecutor | None = None

def get_cpu_executor() -> ThreadPoolExecutor:
    global _executor
    if _executor is None:
        _executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=8, thread_name_prefix="quarry-cpu")
        atexit.register(shutdown_executor)
    return _executor

def shutdown_executor() -> None:
    global _executor
    if _executor is not None:
        _executor.shutdown(wait=True)
        _executor = None
"""PostgreSQL 检索实现。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

from quarry.common.vectors import to_halfvec_literal
from quarry.infra.db import acquire
from quarry.infra.embed import get_embedder
from quarry.infra.executor import get_cpu_executor
from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit
from quarry.retrieval.query import build_tsquery

_SQL_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "sql"
_HYBRID_SQL = (_SQL_DIR / "hybrid_search.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

async def hybrid_search(
    collection_id: UUID,
    query: str,
    caller_labels: list[str],
    meta_filter: dict | None = None,
    dense_k: int = 50,
    sparse_k: int = 50,
    weight_dense: float = 0.5,
    weight_sparse: float = 0.5,
    rrf_k: int = 60,
    top_k: int = 10,
) -> list[Hit]:
    """混合检索，返回按融合分数排序的命中列表。"""
    meta_filter = meta_filter or {}
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()

    # 向量化是网络 I/O，分词是 CPU，两件事同时做。
    # 分词丢进线程池的原因见 4.7。
    qvec_list, tsq = await asyncio.gather(
        get_embedder().embed([query]),
        loop.run_in_executor(get_cpu_executor(), build_tsquery, query),
    )
    vec_lit = to_halfvec_literal(qvec_list[0])

    async with acquire() as conn, conn.transaction():
        # SET LOCAL 只在本事务内生效，不会污染连接池里的这条连接。
        # 为什么要开迭代扫描，见 4.5。
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL hnsw.iterative_scan = relaxed_order")
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL hnsw.max_scan_tuples = 20000")
        rows = await conn.fetch(
            _HYBRID_SQL,
            collection_id, caller_labels, meta_filter, vec_lit, tsq,
            dense_k, sparse_k, weight_dense, weight_sparse, rrf_k, top_k,
        )

    return [Hit(
        chunk_id=r["chunk_id"],
        document_id=r["document_id"],
        parent_id=r["parent_id"],
        heading_path=list(r["heading_path"] or []),
        content=r["content"],
        char_start=r["char_start"],
        char_end=r["char_end"],
        score=float(r["score"]),
        dense_rank=r["dense_rank"],
        sparse_rank=r["sparse_rank"],
        dense_score=float(r["dense_score"]) if r["dense_score"] is not None else None,
        sparse_score=float(r["sparse_score"]) if r["sparse_score"] is not None else None,
    ) for r in rows]

class PgRetriever:
    """把 hybrid_search 包成符合 Retriever 协议的对象。见 4.6。"""

    async def search(
        self,
        collection_id: UUID,
        query: str,
        caller_labels: list[str],
        meta_filter: dict | None = None,
        top_k: int = 10,
    ) -> list[Hit]:
        return await hybrid_search(
            collection_id=collection_id, query=query,
            caller_labels=caller_labels, meta_filter=meta_filter, top_k=top_k)
4.3.8 试跑
"""试跑混合检索，顺带看清两路各自的贡献。"""
import asyncio
import sys

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool
from quarry.retrieval.pg.searcher import hybrid_search

def _f(x: float | None) -> str:
    return "-" if x is None else f"{x:.3f}"

async def main(query: str, slug: str = "travel-guide") -> None:
    await init_pool()
    try:
        async with acquire() as conn:
            coll_id = await conn.fetchval(
                "SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = $1 AND status = 'active'",
                slug)
        if coll_id is None:
            print(f"集合不存在 {slug}")
            return

        hits = await hybrid_search(
            collection_id=coll_id, query=query, caller_labels=["public"])

        print(f"\n查询  {query}")
        print("=" * 64)
        for i, h in enumerate(hits, 1):
            d = h.dense_rank if h.dense_rank is not None else "-"
            s = h.sparse_rank if h.sparse_rank is not None else "-"
            print(f"\n[{i}] 融合 {h.score:.4f}  余弦 {_f(h.dense_score)}  "
                  f"dense 第 {d} 名，sparse 第 {s} 名")
            print(f"    {' > '.join(h.heading_path)}")
            print(f"    {h.content[:60]}...")
    finally:
        await close_pool()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    q = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "兵马俑的门票多少钱"
    slug = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "travel-guide"
    asyncio.run(main(q, slug))

"""权限红线。这些用例必须永远通过，挂了就是权限被绕过，不是功能 bug。"""
import pytest

import quarry.infra.embed as embed
from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool
from quarry.retrieval.pg.searcher import hybrid_search

@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def db_pool():
    """每个用例自己开关一次连接池，并重建向量化客户端。

    别写成 scope="session"：pytest-asyncio 默认每个用例换一个事件循环，
    连接池和向量化客户端里的 HTTP 连接都绑在创建它们的那个循环上，
    沿用到下一个用例会报 attached to a different loop 或 Event loop is closed。
    """
    embed._embedder = None
    await init_pool()
    yield
    await close_pool()

async def _coll_id(slug: str):
    async with acquire() as conn:
        cid = await conn.fetchval(
            "SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = $1 AND status = 'active'", slug)
    assert cid is not None, f"集合 {slug} 不存在，先跑第三章的 ingest_corpus"
    return cid

async def test_public_caller_cannot_see_corp_policy():
    """红线 1  public 调用者看不到企业制度。"""
    hits = await hybrid_search(
        collection_id=await _coll_id("corp-policy"),
        query="住宿标准是多少", caller_labels=["public"])
    assert hits == [], f"权限泄漏：public 拿到了 {len(hits)} 条企业制度"

async def test_corp_caller_can_see_own_content():
    """红线 2  有权限的调用者必须能召回，这是反向验证。"""
    hits = await hybrid_search(
        collection_id=await _coll_id("corp-policy"),
        query="住宿标准是多少", caller_labels=["org:demo-corp"])
    assert hits, "有权限却召回为空，说明过滤写得过严"

async def test_empty_labels_returns_nothing():
    """红线 3  空标签什么都看不到，哪怕查的是公开内容。"""
    hits = await hybrid_search(
        collection_id=await _coll_id("travel-guide"),
        query="兵马俑", caller_labels=[])
    assert hits == [], "空标签必须返回空"

async def test_wrong_org_cannot_see_other_org():
    """红线 4  A 组织看不到 B 组织的内容。"""
    hits = await hybrid_search(
        collection_id=await _coll_id("corp-policy"),
        query="住宿标准是多少", caller_labels=["org:other-corp"])
    assert hits == [], "跨组织泄漏"

async def test_manager_only_content_needs_role():
    """红线 5  内部笔记对 public 不可见。"""
    hits = await hybrid_search(
        collection_id=await _coll_id("internal-notes"),
        query="入职", caller_labels=["public"])
    assert hits == [], "public 不该看到内部笔记"

4.5 强过滤下的召回保障
4.5.1 问题长什么样
4.5.2 动手：把这个问题造出来
"""造一批数据，把强过滤下的漏召回现象跑出来。

思路：给每个块预先分配一个"离查询向量的名次"，把 10 个稀有标签块
均匀撒在第 50～850 名之间。iterative_scan=off 时 HNSW 只拿前 ef_search 个
候选再过滤，所以 ef_search 从 40 调到 1000，返回条数会从 0 逐步补到 10。

做完会把造的数据清掉，不影响你现有的语料。
"""
import asyncio
import math
import random

from quarry.common.vectors import to_halfvec_literal
from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool

N_TOTAL = 5000
DIM = 1024
BATCH = 500
TOP_K = 10

# 稀有标签块的名次（0 起算），名次越小离查询越近
RARE_POSITIONS = [50, 120, 180, 250, 350, 450, 550, 650, 750, 850]
EF_LIST = [40, 100, 150, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900, 1000]

SIM_TOP = 0.95      # 第 0 名与查询的余弦相似度
SIM_SPAN = 0.90     # 最后一名的相似度 = SIM_TOP - SIM_SPAN = 0.05

def unit(vec):
    n = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in vec)) or 1.0
    return [v / n for v in vec]

def make_vec(q, sim, rng):
    """构造一个与 q 的余弦相似度恰好为 sim 的单位向量。"""
    u = [rng.gauss(0, 1) for _ in range(DIM)]
    dot = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(u, q))
    u = unit([a - dot * b for a, b in zip(u, q)])      # 去掉 q 方向的分量
    ortho = math.sqrt(max(0.0, 1 - sim * sim))
    return unit([sim * a + ortho * b for a, b in zip(q, u)])

async def setup(conn):
    tenant_id = await conn.fetchval(
        """INSERT INTO tenants (slug, name) VALUES ('stress', '压测租户')
           ON CONFLICT (slug) DO UPDATE SET name = EXCLUDED.name
           RETURNING id""")
    coll_id = await conn.fetchval(
        """INSERT INTO collections (tenant_id, slug, name, embed_model, embed_dim)
           VALUES ($1, 'stress', '压测集合', 'synthetic', 1024)
           ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, slug) WHERE status = 'active'
           DO UPDATE SET name = EXCLUDED.name
           RETURNING id""", tenant_id)
    doc_id = await conn.fetchval(
        """INSERT INTO documents (collection_id, source_uri, sha256, status)
           VALUES ($1, 'synthetic.md', 'x', 'ready')
           ON CONFLICT (collection_id, source_uri, version)
           DO UPDATE SET status = 'ready'
           RETURNING id""", coll_id)

    await conn.execute("DELETE FROM chunks WHERE collection_id = $1", coll_id)

    rng = random.Random(42)
    q = unit([rng.gauss(0, 1) for _ in range(DIM)])
    rare_set = set(RARE_POSITIONS)

    # 打乱插入顺序，避免"按相似度顺序插入"影响 HNSW 建图
    order = list(range(N_TOTAL))
    rng.shuffle(order)

    sql = """INSERT INTO chunks (chunk_id, document_id, collection_id, doc_version,
                                 level, parent_id, ord, heading_path, content,
                                 char_start, char_end, acl_labels, embedding)
             VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 1, 1, 'synthetic-parent', $4, ARRAY['压测'],
                     $5, 0, 1, $6, $7::halfvec(1024))"""
    batch = []
    for n, pos in enumerate(order, 1):
        sim = SIM_TOP - SIM_SPAN * pos / (N_TOTAL - 1)
        vec = make_vec(q, sim, rng)
        labels = ["org:tiny-team"] if pos in rare_set else ["public"]
        batch.append((f"stress-{pos:06d}", doc_id, coll_id, pos, "x",
                      labels, to_halfvec_literal(vec)))
        if len(batch) >= BATCH:
            await conn.executemany(sql, batch)
            batch.clear()
            print(f"  已写入 {n} / {N_TOTAL}")
    if batch:
        await conn.executemany(sql, batch)

    return coll_id, to_halfvec_literal(q)

PROBE_SQL = """
SELECT chunk_id FROM chunks
WHERE collection_id = $1 AND level = 1
  AND acl_labels && ARRAY['org:tiny-team']::text[]
ORDER BY embedding <=> $2::halfvec(1024)
LIMIT $3
"""

async def exact_rare_ranks(conn, coll_id, qvec):
    """精确计算稀有块在本集合里的名次（1 起算）。"""
    rows = await conn.fetch(
        """WITH ranked AS (
             SELECT acl_labels,
                    row_number() OVER (ORDER BY embedding <=> $2::halfvec(1024)) AS rn
             FROM chunks WHERE collection_id = $1 AND level = 1)
           SELECT rn FROM ranked
           WHERE acl_labels && ARRAY['org:tiny-team']::text[]
           ORDER BY rn""", coll_id, qvec)
    return [r["rn"] for r in rows]

async def probe(conn, coll_id, qvec, scan_mode, ef_search):
    """强制走 HNSW，返回 (实际条数, 扫描方式)。"""
    async with conn.transaction():
        await conn.execute(f"SET LOCAL hnsw.iterative_scan = {scan_mode}")
        await conn.execute(f"SET LOCAL hnsw.ef_search = {ef_search}")
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL hnsw.max_scan_tuples = 20000")
        # 堵死顺序扫描和"GIN 过滤后再排序"这两条路，只剩 HNSW
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL enable_seqscan = off")
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL enable_bitmapscan = off")
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL enable_sort = off")

        plan_rows = await conn.fetch("EXPLAIN " + PROBE_SQL, coll_id, qvec, TOP_K)
        plan = "\n".join(r[0] for r in plan_rows)
        used = "HNSW" if "hnsw" in plan.lower() else "其他"

        rows = await conn.fetch(PROBE_SQL, coll_id, qvec, TOP_K)
    return len(rows), used

async def main():
    await init_pool()
    try:
        async with acquire() as conn:
            try:
                print("造数据中，大约需要一分钟")
                coll_id, qvec = await setup(conn)
                await conn.execute("ANALYZE chunks")
                print(f"造好了，{N_TOTAL} 个块，其中 {len(RARE_POSITIONS)} 个带 org:tiny-team\n")

                ranks = await exact_rare_ranks(conn, coll_id, qvec)
                print(f"稀有块的精确名次：{ranks}\n")

                print(f"{'iterative_scan':<16}{'ef_search':<11}{'扫描':<7}"
                      f"{'理论':<7}{'实际':<7}")
                print("-" * 50)
                for ef in EF_LIST:
                    expect = min(TOP_K, sum(1 for r in ranks if r <= ef))
                    n, used = await probe(conn, coll_id, qvec, "off", ef)
                    bar = "█" * n + "·" * (TOP_K - n)
                    print(f"{'off':<16}{ef:<11}{used:<7}{expect:<7}{n:<7}{bar}")

                print("-" * 50)
                for mode in ("relaxed_order", "strict_order"):
                    n, used = await probe(conn, coll_id, qvec, mode, 40)
                    bar = "█" * n + "·" * (TOP_K - n)
                    print(f"{mode:<16}{40:<11}{used:<7}{TOP_K:<7}{n:<7}{bar}")
            finally:
                await conn.execute("DELETE FROM tenants WHERE slug = 'stress'")
                print("\n压测数据已清理")
    finally:
        await close_pool()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

4.6 检索抽象：一个有争议的设计
4.6.1 问题是什么
调用方 → hybrid_search() → 一条 SQL → 返回 Hit 列表
class Retriever(Protocol):
    async def search(self, collection_id, query, caller_labels,
                     meta_filter=None, top_k=10) -> list[Hit]: ...
4.6.2 支持的两个理由
class FakeRetriever:
    def __init__(self, canned: list[Hit] | None = None):
        self._canned = canned or []

    async def search(self, ...) -> list[Hit]:
        return self._canned[:top_k]
4.6.3 反对的两个理由
4.6.4 三条判据
4.6.5 落地
"""FakeRetriever。给契约测试和离线开发用。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

class FakeRetriever:
    """返回预设的命中列表，不连数据库。"""

    def __init__(self, canned: list[Hit] | None = None):
        self._canned = list(canned or [])
        self.calls: list[dict] = []          # 记下每次调用，方便测试里断言

    async def search(
        self,
        collection_id: UUID,
        query: str,
        caller_labels: list[str],
        meta_filter: dict | None = None,
        top_k: int = 10,
    ) -> list[Hit]:
        self.calls.append({
            "collection_id": collection_id, "query": query,
            "caller_labels": list(caller_labels),
            "meta_filter": dict(meta_filter or {}), "top_k": top_k,
        })
        # 和真实现保持同样的约定：按分数降序、不超过 top_k
        ordered = sorted(self._canned, key=lambda h: h.score, reverse=True)
        return ordered[:top_k]

    def set_hits(self, hits: list[Hit]) -> None:
        self._canned = list(hits)
"""契约测试。任何 Retriever 实现都要满足这几条，包括 Fake。"""
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit
from quarry.retrieval.fake import FakeRetriever

def make_hits(n: int = 5) -> list[Hit]:
    return [Hit(
        chunk_id=f"c{i}", document_id=uuid4(), parent_id=f"p{i}",
        heading_path=["某节"], content=f"内容 {i}",
        char_start=0, char_end=10,
        score=1.0 - i * 0.1,
        dense_rank=i + 1 if i < 3 else None,
        sparse_rank=i + 1 if i > 1 else None,
        dense_score=0.9 - i * 0.1 if i < 3 else None,
        sparse_score=0.2 - i * 0.01 if i > 1 else None,
    ) for i in range(n)]

@pytest.fixture
def retriever():
    """想把这套契约用在 PgRetriever 上，改这一个 fixture 就行。"""
    return FakeRetriever(make_hits())

async def test_never_returns_more_than_top_k(retriever):
    result = await retriever.search(uuid4(), "测试", ["public"], top_k=3)
    assert len(result) <= 3

async def test_results_are_sorted_by_score_desc(retriever):
    result = await retriever.search(uuid4(), "测试", ["public"], top_k=5)
    scores = [h.score for h in result]
    assert scores == sorted(scores, reverse=True)

async def test_every_hit_has_usable_evidence_fields(retriever):
    """第一章说过，一份证据的最小定义有五样东西，少一样上游就用不了。"""
    for h in await retriever.search(uuid4(), "测试", ["public"]):
        assert h.chunk_id
        assert isinstance(h.content, str) and h.content
        assert h.heading_path is not None
        assert h.char_end > h.char_start
        assert h.score is not None

async def test_at_least_one_rank_is_present(retriever):
    """一条命中至少要被某一路召回过，两路都是 None 说明融合写错了。"""
    for h in await retriever.search(uuid4(), "测试", ["public"]):
        assert h.dense_rank is not None or h.sparse_rank is not None

4.7.3 压测：自己量一次
"""压测混合检索，看并发上升时 QPS 和延迟怎么走。

默认缓存查询向量：每个查询只真实调用一次 embedding，
压测期间不再请求向量化接口，测到的是 jieba + PG 检索的性能。
加 --no-cache 可测端到端耗时（注意接口限流，并发别太高）。
"""
import argparse
import asyncio
import math
import statistics
import sys
import time

from openai.resources.embeddings import AsyncEmbeddings

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool
from quarry.retrieval.pg.searcher import hybrid_search

QUERIES = [
    "兵马俑的门票多少钱", "从机场到市区怎么走", "有什么本地小吃",
    "开放时间是几点", "需要提前预约吗", "住宿大概什么价位",
]

# ---------------- embedding 缓存 ----------------
# 只在压测脚本里临时替换 openai 客户端的 create 方法，不影响业务代码
_orig_create = AsyncEmbeddings.create
_cache: dict[str, object] = {}
_stats = {"hit": 0, "miss": 0}

async def _cached_create(self, *args, **kwargs):
    key = repr((args, sorted(kwargs.items())))
    if key in _cache:
        _stats["hit"] += 1
        return _cache[key]
    _stats["miss"] += 1
    resp = await _orig_create(self, *args, **kwargs)
    _cache[key] = resp
    return resp

def enable_embed_cache():
    AsyncEmbeddings.create = _cached_create

# ---------------- 压测 ----------------
def pct(sorted_vals: list[float], p: float) -> float:
    idx = max(0, math.ceil(len(sorted_vals) * p) - 1)
    return sorted_vals[idx]

async def bench(coll_id, concurrency: int, total: int):
    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(concurrency)
    latencies: list[float] = []
    errors = 0

    async def one(i: int):
        nonlocal errors
        async with sem:
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            try:
                await hybrid_search(collection_id=coll_id,
                                    query=QUERIES[i % len(QUERIES)],
                                    caller_labels=["public"], top_k=10)
            except Exception as e:
                errors += 1
                if errors == 1:
                    print(f"  首个错误：{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:120]}",
                          file=sys.stderr)
                return
            latencies.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)

    start = time.perf_counter()
    await asyncio.gather(*[one(i) for i in range(total)])
    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start

    ok = len(latencies)
    if not ok:
        print(f"并发 {concurrency:<4} 全部失败（{errors} 个）")
        return
    latencies.sort()
    print(f"并发 {concurrency:<4} 成功 {ok:<5} 失败 {errors:<4} 耗时 {elapsed:5.2f}s  "
          f"QPS {ok / elapsed:7.1f}  p50 {statistics.median(latencies):7.1f}ms  "
          f"p95 {pct(latencies, 0.95):7.1f}ms  p99 {pct(latencies, 0.99):7.1f}ms")

async def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--no-cache", action="store_true", help="不缓存 embedding，测端到端")
    parser.add_argument("--total", type=int, default=300, help="每档请求数")
    parser.add_argument("--levels", default="1,5,10,20,50", help="并发档位，逗号分隔")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    levels = [int(x) for x in args.levels.split(",")]

    if not args.no_cache:
        enable_embed_cache()

    await init_pool()
    try:
        async with acquire() as conn:
            coll_id = await conn.fetchval(
                "SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = 'travel-guide' AND status = 'active'")
        if coll_id is None:
            print("找不到 slug = 'travel-guide' 的活跃集合", file=sys.stderr)
            return

        # 预热：填满缓存，同时让连接池、jieba 词典、PG 缓存都热起来
        for q in QUERIES:
            await hybrid_search(collection_id=coll_id, query=q,
                                caller_labels=["public"], top_k=10)
        mode = "端到端（含 embedding）" if args.no_cache else "已缓存 embedding（只测检索）"
        print(f"模式：{mode}，预热完成\n")

        for c in levels:
            await bench(coll_id, concurrency=c, total=args.total)

        if not args.no_cache:
            print(f"\nembedding 真实调用 {_stats['miss']} 次，缓存命中 {_stats['hit']} 次")
    finally:
        await close_pool()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

4.8 检索调试台
4.8.1 页面长什么样
4.8.2 后端：调试专用接口
-- 调试用。和 hybrid_search.sql 的前三段完全一致，
-- 区别只在于把三路结果都带回去，而不是只返回融合后的 top_k。
-- 参数含义见 hybrid_search.sql，$11 在这里是每一列展示多少条。

WITH dense AS (
  SELECT chunk_id, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY dist) AS rk, dist
  FROM (
    SELECT chunk_id, embedding <=> $4::halfvec(1024) AS dist
    FROM chunks
    WHERE collection_id = $1 AND level = 1
      AND acl_labels && $2::text[] AND meta @> $3::jsonb
    ORDER BY embedding <=> $4::halfvec(1024)
    LIMIT $6
  ) d
),
sparse AS (
  SELECT chunk_id, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY ts_score DESC) AS rk, ts_score
  FROM (
    SELECT chunk_id, ts_rank_cd(tokens, to_tsquery('simple', $5)) AS ts_score
    FROM chunks
    WHERE collection_id = $1 AND level = 1
      AND acl_labels && $2::text[] AND meta @> $3::jsonb
      AND tokens @@ to_tsquery('simple', $5)
    ORDER BY ts_rank_cd(tokens, to_tsquery('simple', $5)) DESC
    LIMIT $7
  ) s
),
merged AS (
  SELECT chunk_id,
         COALESCE($8::float8 / ($10::int + d.rk), 0)
       + COALESCE($9::float8 / ($10::int + s.rk), 0) AS score,
         d.rk AS dense_rank, s.rk AS sparse_rank
  FROM dense d FULL OUTER JOIN sparse s USING (chunk_id)
),
dense_rows AS (
  SELECT d.rk, round((1 - d.dist)::numeric, 4) AS score,
         c.chunk_id, c.heading_path, left(c.content, 70) AS preview
  FROM dense d
  JOIN chunks c ON c.collection_id = $1 AND c.chunk_id = d.chunk_id
),
sparse_rows AS (
  SELECT s.rk, round(s.ts_score::numeric, 4) AS score,
         c.chunk_id, c.heading_path, left(c.content, 70) AS preview
  FROM sparse s
  JOIN chunks c ON c.collection_id = $1 AND c.chunk_id = s.chunk_id
),
merged_rows AS (
  SELECT row_number() OVER (ORDER BY m.score DESC) AS rk,
         round(m.score::numeric, 5) AS score,
         m.dense_rank, m.sparse_rank,
         c.chunk_id, c.heading_path, left(c.content, 70) AS preview
  FROM merged m
  JOIN chunks c ON c.collection_id = $1 AND c.chunk_id = m.chunk_id
  ORDER BY m.score DESC
  LIMIT $11
)
SELECT
  (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x) ORDER BY x.rk) FROM dense_rows  x) AS dense,
  (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x) ORDER BY x.rk) FROM sparse_rows x) AS sparse,
  (SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x) ORDER BY x.rk) FROM merged_rows x) AS merged;
"""检索调试接口。只在开发环境开放。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import time
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException

from quarry.common.vectors import to_halfvec_literal
from quarry.infra.db import acquire
from quarry.infra.embed import get_embedder
from quarry.infra.executor import get_cpu_executor
from quarry.infra.settings import get_settings
from quarry.retrieval.query import build_tsquery

router = APIRouter(prefix="/debug", tags=["debug"])

_SQL = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "retrieval" / "pg" / "sql" / "debug_search.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

@router.post("/search")
async def debug_search(payload: dict):
    """返回三路结果加耗时归因。"""
    if get_settings().app_env != "development":
        raise HTTPException(403, "调试接口仅开发环境可用")

    query = (payload.get("query") or "").strip()
    if not query:
        raise HTTPException(400, "query 不能为空")

    slug = payload.get("collection", "travel-guide")
    labels = payload.get("caller_labels") or ["public"]
    meta_filter = payload.get("meta_filter") or {}
    top_k = int(payload.get("top_k", 10))
    w_dense = float(payload.get("weight_dense", 0.5))
    w_sparse = float(payload.get("weight_sparse", 0.5))
    rrf_k = int(payload.get("rrf_k", 60))

    timings: dict[str, float] = {}
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    async with acquire() as conn:
        coll_id = await conn.fetchval(
            "SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = $1 AND status = 'active'", slug)
    if coll_id is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, f"集合不存在 {slug}")
    timings["lookup_ms"] = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    qvec_list, tsq = await asyncio.gather(
        get_embedder().embed([query]),
        loop.run_in_executor(get_cpu_executor(), build_tsquery, query),
    )
    timings["prepare_ms"] = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    async with acquire() as conn, conn.transaction():
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL hnsw.iterative_scan = relaxed_order")
        await conn.execute("SET LOCAL hnsw.max_scan_tuples = 20000")
        row = await conn.fetchrow(
            _SQL, coll_id, labels, meta_filter,
            to_halfvec_literal(qvec_list[0]), tsq,
            50, 50, w_dense, w_sparse, rrf_k, top_k)
    timings["sql_ms"] = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
    timings["total_ms"] = sum(timings.values())

    return {
        "query": query,
        "tsquery": tsq,                 # 把拼出来的 tsquery 也显示出来
        "dense": row["dense"] or [],
        "sparse": row["sparse"] or [],
        "merged": row["merged"] or [],
        "timings": timings,
    }

  .timing-title { margin: 0 0 10px; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--brand-strong); }
  .timing-rows {
    display: grid;

  @keyframes grow { from { transform: scaleX(0); } }

  /* ── 响应式 ─────────────────────────────────── */

<script>
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

const COLS = [
  { key: 'dense',  label: 'Dense',  body: 'col-dense',  count: 'n-dense',
    idle: '按回车或点「搜索」开始检索',
    none: '没有命中，检查集合和权限标签' },
  { key: 'sparse', label: 'Sparse', body: 'col-sparse', count: 'n-sparse',
    idle: '关键词召回的结果显示在这里',
    none: '没有关键词命中，对照上方 tsquery 排查' },
  { key: 'merged', label: 'RRF',    body: 'col-merged', count: 'n-merged',
    idle: '两路融合后的排序显示在这里',
    none: '两路都没有结果' },
];

const STAGES = [
  ['lookup_ms',  '查集合'],
  ['prepare_ms', '向量化+分词'],
  ['sql_ms',     'SQL'],
];

const SMOOTH = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth';

let lastData = null;   // 最近一次成功的响应
let selected = null;   // { cid, source }；重新搜索后该块若仍在结果里就保持选中，方便对比调权重前后的名次
let reqSeq = 0;        // 只渲染最后一次请求的结果
let inflight = null;

async function search() {
  const body = {
    query: $('q').value,
    collection: $('coll').value,
    caller_labels: $('labels').value
                     .split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean),
    top_k: parseInt($('topk').value, 10),
    weight_dense: parseFloat($('wd').value),
    weight_sparse: parseFloat($('ws').value),
  };
  const weights = `${$('wd').value.trim()} / ${$('ws').value.trim()}`;

  if (inflight) inflight.abort();
  const ctrl = inflight = new AbortController();
  const seq = ++reqSeq;
  setBusy(true);

  let data;
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:8100/debug/search', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal: ctrl.signal,
    });
    const raw = await resp.text();
    if (!resp.ok) {
      if (seq === reqSeq) showError(`HTTP ${resp.status}${raw.trim() ? '：' + raw.trim() : ''}`);
      return;
    }
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      if (seq === reqSeq) showError(`响应不是合法的 JSON：${raw.slice(0, 300)}`);
      return;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (seq === reqSeq) {
      showError(`请求没有完成（${err && err.message ? err.message : err}），确认服务已启动后重试`);
    }
    return;
  } finally {
    if (seq === reqSeq) { setBusy(false); inflight = null; }
  }
  if (seq !== reqSeq) return;

  lastData = data;
  showTsquery(data.tsquery);
  COLS.forEach(c => renderColumn(c, listOf(c.key)));
  $('w-sub').textContent = weights;
  renderTimings(data.timings);

  const again = selected && findHit(selected.cid, selected.source);
  if (again) choose(again.hit, again.source);
  else clearSelection();

  const total = num(data.timings && data.timings.total_ms);
  setStatus('ok', total === null ? '完成' : `完成，用时 ${total.toFixed(1)} ms`);
}

/* ── 状态 ─────────────────────────────────────── */

function setBusy(on) {
  const btn = $('go');
  btn.textContent = on ? '检索中…' : '搜索';
  btn.setAttribute('aria-busy', String(on));
  $('columns').classList.toggle('is-busy', on);
  if (on) setStatus('busy', '检索中…');
}

function setStatus(kind, text) {
  $('status').dataset.kind = kind;
  $('status-text').textContent = text;
}

function showTsquery(tsq) {
  $('tsq').classList.remove('is-error');
  $('tsq-tag').textContent = 'tsquery';
  $('columns').classList.remove('is-stale');
  const v = $('tsq-val');
  if (tsq === null || tsq === undefined || tsq === '') {
    v.innerHTML = '<span class="muted">（空）</span>';
  } else {
    v.textContent = String(tsq);
  }
}

// 出错时保留上一次结果，但整体压暗，避免误读
function showError(msg) {
  $('tsq').classList.add('is-error');
  $('tsq-tag').textContent = '出错';
  $('tsq-val').textContent = msg;
  $('columns').classList.add('is-stale');
  setStatus('err', '请求失败');
}

/* ── 结果列 ───────────────────────────────────── */

function listOf(key) {
  const v = lastData && lastData[key];
  return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
}

function renderColumn(c, hits) {
  const box = $(c.body);
  const n = $(c.count);
  n.textContent = hits.length;
  n.hidden = false;
  // 同一列统一小数位：最大分数 ≥10（BM25 一类）保留 2 位，否则 4 位（相似度、距离、RRF 不丢精度）
  const digits = hits.some(h => Math.abs(num(h.score) ?? 0) >= 10) ? 2 : 4;
  box.replaceChildren(...(hits.length ? hits.map(h => card(h, c.key, digits)) : [emptyBox(c.none)]));
  box.scrollTop = 0;
}

function emptyBox(text) {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'empty';
  d.textContent = text;
  return d;
}

function card(h, source, digits) {
  const cid = String(h.chunk_id);   // 统一成字符串，数字 id 也能和 dataset 对上
  const path = pathOf(h);
  const el = document.createElement('article');
  el.className = 'hit';
  el.tabIndex = 0;
  el.dataset.cid = cid;
  el.innerHTML =
      `<div class="hit-top"><span class="rk">#${esc(h.rk)}</span>`
    + `<span class="sc" title="${esc(h.score)}">${fmtScore(h.score, digits)}</span></div>`
    + `<p class="hit-text">${h.preview ? esc(h.preview) : '<span class="muted">（无预览）</span>'}</p>`
    + `<p class="hit-path" title="${esc(path)}">${path ? esc(path) : '—'}</p>`;
  el.addEventListener('click', () => choose(h, source));
  el.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); choose(h, source); }
  });
  el.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => link(cid));
  el.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => link(null));
  el.addEventListener('focus', () => link(cid));
  el.addEventListener('blur', () => link(null));
  return el;
}

// 同一个块在三列里一起高亮，这是这个页面最有用的一个交互
function choose(h, source) {
  const cid = String(h.chunk_id);
  selected = { cid, source };
  document.querySelectorAll('.hit').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('is-selected', el.dataset.cid === cid);
  });
  COLS.forEach(c => {
    const box = $(c.body);
    const el = box.querySelector('.hit.is-selected');
    if (el) reveal(box, el);
  });
  renderDetail(h, source);
}

// 悬停时的临时联动（沙色），不改变选中
function link(cid) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.hit').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('is-linked', cid !== null && el.dataset.cid === cid);
  });
}

function clearSelection() {
  selected = null;
  document.querySelectorAll('.hit.is-selected').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('is-selected'));
  $('detail-sub').textContent = '未选中';
  $('detail-body').innerHTML =
    '<p class="detail-empty">点击左侧任意一条，三列中的同一个块会一起高亮。</p>';
}

// 选中块在其他列里被滚出视野时滚回来，只滚列内，不动整页
function reveal(box, el) {
  const top = el.offsetTop;
  const bottom = top + el.offsetHeight;
  if (top < box.scrollTop) {
    box.scrollTo({ top: top - 6, behavior: SMOOTH });
  } else if (bottom > box.scrollTop + box.clientHeight) {
    box.scrollTo({ top: bottom - box.clientHeight + 6, behavior: SMOOTH });
  }
}

function lookup(key, cid) {
  return listOf(key).find(x => String(x.chunk_id) === cid) || null;
}

function findHit(cid, prefer) {
  for (const key of [prefer, 'merged', 'dense', 'sparse']) {
    const hit = lookup(key, cid);
    if (hit) return { hit, source: key };
  }
  return null;
}

/* ── 详情 ─────────────────────────────────────── */

function renderDetail(h, source) {
  const cid = String(h.chunk_id);
  const d = lookup('dense', cid);
  const s = lookup('sparse', cid);
  const m = lookup('merged', cid);
  const col = COLS.find(c => c.key === source);
  $('detail-sub').textContent = `选中 ${col ? col.label : ''} #${h.rk ?? '?'}`;

  const NA = '<span class="na">未上榜</span>';
  const DASH = '<span class="na">—</span>';
  const has = (v) => v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== '';
  const rows = [];
  const row = (key, html, { src = false, txt = false } = {}) => rows.push(
    `<div class="kv-row${src ? ' is-src' : ''}"><dt>${key}</dt>`
    + `<dd${txt ? ' class="txt"' : ''}>${html}</dd></div>`);

  row('chunk_id', esc(cid));
  // 以下三个字段接口返回了才显示
  if (has(h.collection_id)) row('collection_id', esc(h.collection_id));
  if (has(h.level)) row('level', esc(h.level));
  const path = pathOf(h);
  row('heading_path', path ? esc(path) : DASH, { txt: true });

  // 名次优先用接口给的 dense_rank / sparse_rank，没有再从对应列表里找
  const dRank = h.dense_rank ?? (d && d.rk);
  const sRank = h.sparse_rank ?? (s && s.rk);
  row('dense_rank',   has(dRank) ? esc(dRank) : NA,    { src: source === 'dense' });
  row('dense_score',  d ? esc(d.score) : DASH,         { src: source === 'dense' });
  row('sparse_rank',  has(sRank) ? esc(sRank) : NA,    { src: source === 'sparse' });
  row('sparse_score', s ? esc(s.score) : DASH,         { src: source === 'sparse' });
  row('rrf_rank',     m && has(m.rk) ? esc(m.rk) : NA, { src: source === 'merged' });
  row('rrf_score',    m ? esc(m.score) : DASH,         { src: source === 'merged' });

  if (has(h.acl_labels)) {
    const acl = Array.isArray(h.acl_labels) ? h.acl_labels.join(', ') : String(h.acl_labels);
    row('acl_labels', acl ? esc(acl) : DASH);
  }

  const preview = has(h.preview)
    ? `${esc(h.preview)}<span class="ell">…</span>`
    : '<span class="muted">—</span>';

  $('detail-body').innerHTML =
      `<dl class="kv">${rows.join('')}</dl>`
    + `<div class="preview"><div class="preview-label">preview</div><p>${preview}</p></div>`;
}

function renderTimings(t) {
  const box = $('timing-rows');
  if (!t) {
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="timing-empty">搜索后显示各阶段耗时</span>';
    return;
  }
  const total = num(t.total_ms);
  let html = '';
  for (const [key, label] of STAGES) html += barRow(label, num(t[key]), total, '');
  html += barRow('总计', total, total, ' is-total');
  box.innerHTML = html;
}

function barRow(label, v, total, cls) {
  const pct = v !== null && total ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, v / total * 100)) : 0;
  const width = v > 0 ? `max(3px, ${pct.toFixed(2)}%)` : '0';
  return `<span class="bar-label${cls}">${label}</span>`
       + `<span class="bar${cls}"><i style="width:${width}"></i></span>`
       + `<span class="bar-val${cls}">${v === null ? '—' : v.toFixed(1)}</span>`;
}

/* ── 工具函数 ─────────────────────────────────── */

function pathOf(h) {
  const p = h.heading_path;
  if (Array.isArray(p)) return p.filter(x => x !== null && x !== '').join(' / ');
  return p ? String(p) : '';
}

function num(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return null;
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

// 卡片里显示的分数；完整值在悬停提示和详情里
function fmtScore(v, digits = 4) {
  const n = num(v);
  if (n === null) return '—';
  if (n !== 0 && Math.abs(n) < 0.5 * 10 ** -digits) return n.toExponential(2);
  return n.toFixed(digits);
}

function esc(v) {
  return String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => (
    { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
}

/* ── 初始化 ───────────────────────────────────── */

function syncColl() { $('coll-view').textContent = $('coll').value; }

$('toolbar').addEventListener('submit', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); search(); });
$('coll').addEventListener('change', syncColl);
window.addEventListener('pageshow', syncColl);
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Escape' && selected) clearSelection();
});

syncColl();
$('w-sub').textContent = `${$('wd').value} / ${$('ws').value}`;
COLS.forEach(c => $(c.body).replaceChildren(emptyBox(c.idle)));
clearSelection();
renderTimings(null);
</script>
</body>
</html>

5.1 准备工作
5.1.1 分段计时工具
"""分段计时。全项目只有这一份。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time
from contextlib import contextmanager

class StageTimer:
    """记录链路各阶段的耗时，单位毫秒。

        t = StageTimer()
        with t.stage("recall"):
            ...
        t.as_dict()        # {"recall_ms": 71.3, "total_ms": 72.0}
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._t0 = time.perf_counter()
        self.stages: dict[str, float] = {}

    @contextmanager
    def stage(self, name: str):
        start = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            # 同名阶段进入多次就累加；中间抛异常也照样记下耗时
            cost = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000
            self.stages[name] = self.stages.get(name, 0.0) + cost

    def mark_total(self) -> None:
        # total 单独测，不从各段相加：并发执行的段加起来会比实际耗时长
        self.stages["total"] = (time.perf_counter() - self._t0) * 1000

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        if "total" not in self.stages:
            self.mark_total()
        return {f"{k}_ms": round(v, 1) for k, v in self.stages.items()}
import time

from quarry.common.timing import StageTimer

t = StageTimer()
with t.stage("sleep"):
    time.sleep(0.05)
print(t.as_dict())
uv run python tmp_timing.py

5.1.2 补配置
# ---------- 重排（第五章） ----------
    rerank_backend: Literal["api", "noop"] = "noop"
    rerank_model: str = ""
    rerank_url: str = ""
    rerank_api_key: SecretStr = SecretStr("")      # 留空 = 复用 LLM_API_KEY
    rerank_candidates: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=100)
    rerank_timeout_s: float = Field(default=3.0, gt=0)

    # ---------- 上下文（第五章） ----------
    context_max_chars: int = Field(default=6000, ge=500, le=50000)
    neighbor_radius: int = Field(default=1, ge=0, le=3)
def switches(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """给 /healthz 回显用。只返回开关与参数，不返回地址和密钥。"""
        return {
            "embed_backend": self.embed_backend,
            "embed_model": self.model_embed,
            "embed_dim": self.embed_dim,
            "rerank_backend": self.rerank_backend,
            "rerank_model": self.rerank_model if self.rerank_backend == "api" else None,
            "rerank_candidates": self.rerank_candidates,
            "context_max_chars": self.context_max_chars,
            "neighbor_radius": self.neighbor_radius,
            "default_top_k": self.default_top_k,
        }
# ============ 重排（第五章） ============

NEIGHBOR_RADIUS=1

5.2 接入重排
5.2.1 重排是干什么的
5.2.2 Reranker 协议与空实现
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path quarry/rerank | Out-Null
"""Reranker 协议。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol

from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

class Reranker(Protocol):
    """所有重排实现都要守住三条：

    1. 只调整顺序，不修改命中的内容与区间；返回不超过 top_k 条。
    2. 真的重排过，才把分数写进 hit.rerank_score；没重排过就保持 None。
    3. 不许抛异常。接口失败时写一条 warning，按原顺序返回前 top_k 条。
    """

    name: str

    async def rerank(self, query: str, hits: list[Hit], top_k: int) -> list[Hit]: ...
"""空实现。对照组，也是默认档。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

class NoopReranker:
    """什么都不做，按原顺序返回前 top_k 条。

    它存在的理由不是"还没实现"，是"需要一个对照组"。
    5.3 的开关对照、第七章的对照实验，都要拿它当基准。
    """

    name = "noop"

    async def rerank(self, query: str, hits: list[Hit],
                     top_k: int) -> list[Hit]:
        return hits[:top_k]        # rerank_score 保持 None，见协议第 2 条

5.2.4 托管接口实现
"""托管重排接口，默认对接百炼 qwen3-rerank。

只要接口的请求是 {model, query, documents, top_n}、
响应是 {results: [{index, relevance_score}]}，换厂商只需要改 .env。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import httpx

from quarry.infra.logging import get_logger
from quarry.infra.settings import Settings
from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

log = get_logger()

MAX_DOC_CHARS = 1000        # 单条候选最多送多少字，防止超过接口的单条长度上限

class ApiReranker:
    name = "api"

    def __init__(self, s: Settings):
        # RERANK_API_KEY 留空时复用 LLM_API_KEY：百炼的重排和 embedding 是同一个 key
        key = s.rerank_api_key.get_secret_value() or s.llm_api_key.get_secret_value()
        if not s.rerank_url or not s.rerank_model:
            raise ValueError("RERANK_BACKEND=api 时必须配置 RERANK_URL 和 RERANK_MODEL")
        if not key or "REPLACE-ME" in key:
            raise ValueError("没有可用的 API Key：先填好 LLM_API_KEY，或单独填 RERANK_API_KEY")

        self._url = s.rerank_url
        self._model = s.rerank_model
        self._timeout = s.rerank_timeout_s
        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
            timeout=s.rerank_timeout_s,
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {key}"},
        )

    async def rerank(self, query: str, hits: list[Hit], top_k: int) -> list[Hit]:
        # 空文本不送：有的服务端会悄悄丢掉空文档，返回的 index 就对不上了
        usable = [h for h in hits if h.content.strip()]
        if not usable:
            return hits[:top_k]

        body = {
            "model": self._model,
            "query": query,
            "documents": [_doc_text(h) for h in usable],
            "top_n": len(usable),
        }
        try:
            async with asyncio.timeout(self._timeout):
                resp = await self._client.post(self._url, json=body)
            if resp.status_code != 200:
                log.warning("重排接口返回 {}：{}", resp.status_code, resp.text[:300])
                return hits[:top_k]
            scores = _parse(resp.json(), len(usable))
        except Exception as e:                  # 协议第 3 条：不许把异常抛出去
            log.warning("重排请求失败：{!r}", e)
            return hits[:top_k]

        if not scores:
            log.warning("重排接口没有返回可用结果：{}", resp.text[:300])
            return hits[:top_k]

        for idx, score in scores.items():
            usable[idx].rerank_score = score
        ranked = sorted((usable[i] for i in scores),
                        key=lambda h: h.rerank_score, reverse=True)
        return ranked[:top_k]

def _parse(data: dict, n: int) -> dict[int, float]:
    """把响应解析成 {在输入里的位置: 分数}。换一个响应格式不同的厂商，只改这个函数。"""
    out: dict[int, float] = {}
    for item in data.get("results") or []:
        idx, score = item.get("index"), item.get("relevance_score")
        if isinstance(idx, int) and 0 <= idx < n and score is not None:
            out[idx] = float(score)
    return out

def _doc_text(hit: Hit) -> str:
    """送去重排的文本 = 标题路径 + 正文。孤零零一句"门票 120 元"，模型不知道说的是哪个景点。"""
    crumb = " > ".join(hit.heading_path)
    body = hit.content[:MAX_DOC_CHARS]
    return f"{crumb}\n{body}" if crumb else body
5.2.5 重排工厂
"""重排工厂：按 RERANK_BACKEND 构造，全进程共用一个。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from quarry.infra.logging import get_logger
from quarry.infra.settings import get_settings
from quarry.rerank.api import ApiReranker
from quarry.rerank.base import Reranker
from quarry.rerank.noop import NoopReranker

log = get_logger()

_reranker: Reranker | None = None

def get_reranker() -> Reranker:
    global _reranker
    if _reranker is None:
        s = get_settings()
        _reranker = ApiReranker(s) if s.rerank_backend == "api" else NoopReranker()
        log.info("重排后端：{}", _reranker.name)
    return _reranker

def reset_reranker() -> None:
    """测试里切换配置、或者每个用例换了事件循环时调用。"""
    global _reranker
    _reranker = None

__all__ = ["Reranker", "get_reranker", "reset_reranker"]
5.2.6 冒烟测试：确认真的在重排
"""重排冒烟测试：三段文本里只有一段相关，而且故意不放在第一位。

通过条件：真的重排过，并且相关的那段被排到了第一。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import sys
from uuid import uuid4

from quarry.infra.settings import get_settings
from quarry.rerank import get_reranker
from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

QUERY = "兵马俑博物馆的门票多少钱"
DOCS = [
    (["科技"], "量子计算是计算科学的一个前沿领域。"),
    (["西安", "兵马俑"], "兵马俑博物馆门票 120 元，开放时间 8:30 至 18:00。"),
    (["西安", "美食"], "回民街人均消费约 60 元，晚上八点后最热闹。"),
]

def make_hit(i: int, path: list[str], text: str) -> Hit:
    return Hit(
        chunk_id=f"smoke-{i}", document_id=uuid4(), parent_id=None,
        heading_path=path, content=text, char_start=0, char_end=len(text),
        score=0.0, dense_rank=i + 1, sparse_rank=None,
        dense_score=None, sparse_score=None,
    )

async def main() -> int:
    if get_settings().rerank_backend != "api":
        print("RERANK_BACKEND 不是 api，先按 5.2.3 改好 .env")
        return 1

    reranker = get_reranker()                  # 缺配置会在这里直接报错
    hits = [make_hit(i, path, text) for i, (path, text) in enumerate(DOCS)]
    ranked = await reranker.rerank(QUERY, hits, top_k=3)

    for h in ranked:
        score = "None  " if h.rerank_score is None else f"{h.rerank_score:.4f}"
        print(f"  {score}  {h.content}")

    if not ranked or ranked[0].rerank_score is None:
        print("❌ 没有真正重排：接口调用失败，已按原顺序返回。往上看日志里的 warning。")
        return 1
    if "兵马俑" not in ranked[0].content:
        print("❌ 接口通了，但相关的那段没有排第一。检查 RERANK_MODEL 是不是重排模型。")
        return 1
    print("✅ 重排接口可用")
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(asyncio.run(main()))

5.2.7 常见报错对照
5.3 对比重排开和关
"""重排开 / 关对照：同一批候选，重排后的第 1 名原来排第几、多花了多少时间。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import statistics
import time

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool
from quarry.infra.settings import get_settings
from quarry.rerank import get_reranker
from quarry.retrieval.pg.searcher import hybrid_search

QUERIES = [
    "兵马俑的门票多少钱",
    "从机场到市区怎么走",
    "有什么本地小吃值得尝",
    "景区几点开门",
    "需要提前预约吗",
    "住在哪个区域比较方便",
    "冬天去合适吗",
    "有没有适合带孩子去的地方",
    "当地交通卡怎么买",
    "去下一个城市坐什么车",
]

async def main() -> None:
    s = get_settings()
    reranker = get_reranker()
    if reranker.name == "noop":
        print("RERANK_BACKEND=noop，重排前后会一模一样。先按 5.2.3 改成 api。")
        return

    await init_pool()
    try:
        async with acquire() as conn:
            coll_id = await conn.fetchval(
                "SELECT id FROM collections WHERE slug = 'travel-guide' AND status = 'active'")
        if coll_id is None:
            print("集合 travel-guide 不存在，先跑第三章的 ingest_corpus")
            return

        print(f"候选数 {s.rerank_candidates}\n")
        print("原排名  第1名  重排耗时   查询")
        positions: list[int] = []
        costs: list[float] = []

        for q in QUERIES:
            hits = await hybrid_search(collection_id=coll_id, query=q,
                                       caller_labels=["public"],
                                       top_k=s.rerank_candidates)
            if not hits:
                print(f"   -     -          -   {q}（没有召回）")
                continue
            before = [h.chunk_id for h in hits]

            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            ranked = await reranker.rerank(q, hits, top_k=len(hits))
            cost = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000

            # 闸门：没有真正重排过，这张表就没有意义
            if ranked[0].rerank_score is None:
                print(f"\n❌ 「{q}」没有真正重排，对照结果作废。先跑 verify_rerank 排查。")
                return

            pos = before.index(ranked[0].chunk_id) + 1
            positions.append(pos)
            costs.append(cost)
            print(f"{pos:>4}    {'换了' if pos != 1 else '没换'}   {cost:6.0f} ms   {q}")

        if positions:
            changed = sum(1 for p in positions if p != 1)
            print("\n" + "=" * 48)
            print(f"第 1 名被换掉    {changed}/{len(positions)}")
            print(f"重排耗时中位数  {statistics.median(costs):.0f} ms")
    finally:
        await close_pool()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

def downgrade() -> None:

        "CREATE INDEX chunks_neighbors ON chunks (document_id, heading_path, ord)"
        " WHERE level = 0;"
    )

5.4.3 证据与引用的数据结构
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path quarry/context/sql | Out-Null
New-Item -ItemType File -Force -Path quarry/context/__init__.py | Out-Null
"""证据与引用：平台交付给上游的数据形状。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from uuid import UUID

@dataclass
class Citation:
    """一条引用。上游用它渲染来源卡片、跳转原文。"""

    document_id: UUID
    source_uri: str
    title: str | None
    url: str | None                  # 原文链接，来自 documents.meta（第三章的抓取清单写入）
    license: str | None
    heading_path: list[str]
    char_start: int                  # 命中块在原文里的起止位置
    char_end: int
    locator_kind: str                # "char" 精确到字符 | "paragraph" 只到段落 | "unknown"
    verified_at: date | None = None  # 核实日期，第六章事实层填写，本章一律为 None

@dataclass
class Evidence:
    """一条证据 = 命中块 + 上下文 + 引用 + 分数。"""

    chunk_id: str
    text: str                        # 命中块原文，引用区间指向它，用来高亮
    context: str                     # 交给大模型读的上下文
    context_level: str               # "neighbors" 带邻块 | "section" 本节 | "chunk" 只有命中块
    citation: Citation

    score: float                     # 融合分（第四章）
    rerank_score: float | None
    dense_score: float | None
    sparse_score: float | None
    dense_rank: int | None
    sparse_rank: int | None
    sibling_hits: int                # 同一小节里被合并掉的其他命中数
5.4.4 两条回捞 SQL
-- 按 chunk_id 批量取父块。
-- 参数：$1 collection_id  $2 父块 chunk_id 数组  $3 caller_labels
-- char_start 本章用不到，第六章要靠它算"命中块在父块里的偏移"。
SELECT chunk_id, document_id, ord, content, char_start
FROM chunks
WHERE collection_id = $1
  AND chunk_id      = ANY($2::text[])
  AND level         = 0
  AND acl_labels   && $3::text[];
-- 取每个父块前后 radius 个相邻父块（同一文档、ord 相邻），走 0003 迁移建的索引。
-- 参数：$1 collection_id  $2 文档 ID 数组  $3 中心 ord 数组（与 $2 一一对应）
--       $4 radius          $5 caller_labels
SELECT c.chunk_id, c.document_id, c.ord, c.content, t.center_ord
FROM unnest($2::uuid[], $3::int[]) AS t(doc_id, center_ord)
JOIN chunks c
  ON c.collection_id = $1
 AND c.document_id   = t.doc_id
 AND c.level         = 0
 AND c.ord BETWEEN t.center_ord - $4 AND t.center_ord + $4
 AND c.ord <> t.center_ord
WHERE c.acl_labels && $5::text[]
ORDER BY c.document_id, c.ord;
5.4.5 扩展模块
"""上下文扩展：同一小节去重、回捞父块和邻块、按预算挑上下文档位。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

_SQL_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "sql"
_PARENTS_SQL = (_SQL_DIR / "fetch_parents.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
_NEIGHBORS_SQL = (_SQL_DIR / "fetch_neighbors.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

SEP = "\n\n"

def dedupe_by_parent(hits: list[Hit]) -> list[tuple[Hit, int]]:
    """同一父块只留排在最前面的那条，返回 [(命中, 被合并掉的同节命中数)]。

    输入必须已经按最终顺序排好（重排之后），这样留下的就是最相关的那条。
    """
    first: dict[str, Hit] = {}
    merged: dict[str, int] = {}
    for h in hits:
        key = h.parent_id or h.chunk_id
        if key in first:
            merged[key] += 1
        else:
            first[key] = h
            merged[key] = 0
    return [(h, merged[key]) for key, h in first.items()]

async def fetch_parents(conn, collection_id: UUID, parent_ids: list[str | None],
                        caller_labels: list[str]) -> dict[str, dict]:
    """批量取父块，返回 {父块 chunk_id: 行}。"""
    ids = list(dict.fromkeys(p for p in parent_ids if p))
    if not ids:
        return {}
    rows = await conn.fetch(_PARENTS_SQL, collection_id, ids, caller_labels)
    return {r["chunk_id"]: dict(r) for r in rows}

async def fetch_neighbors(conn, collection_id: UUID, parents: list[dict], radius: int,
                          caller_labels: list[str]) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
    """批量取每个父块前后 radius 个相邻父块，返回 {父块 chunk_id: [邻块行, ...]}。"""
    if radius <= 0 or not parents:
        return {}
    rows = await conn.fetch(
        _NEIGHBORS_SQL, collection_id,
        [p["document_id"] for p in parents], [p["ord"] for p in parents],
        radius, caller_labels,
    )
    owner = {(p["document_id"], p["ord"]): p["chunk_id"] for p in parents}
    out: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for r in rows:                              # SQL 已经按 ord 排好序
        out.setdefault(owner[(r["document_id"], r["center_ord"])], []).append(dict(r))
    return out

def context_options(parent: dict | None, neighbors: list[dict],
                    chunk_text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """一条证据的上下文，从大到小三档：带邻块 → 只有本节 → 只有命中块。"""
    options: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    if parent is not None:
        before = [n["content"] for n in neighbors if n["ord"] < parent["ord"]]
        after = [n["content"] for n in neighbors if n["ord"] > parent["ord"]]
        if before or after:
            options.append(("neighbors", SEP.join([*before, parent["content"], *after])))
        options.append(("section", parent["content"]))
    options.append(("chunk", chunk_text))
    return options

def apply_budget(all_options: list[list[tuple[str, str]]],
                 max_chars: int) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """按顺序给每条证据挑一档放得下的上下文；连最小一档都放不下就停。

    第一条证据一定保留。每一档都包含命中块本身，所以降档不会丢掉证据。
    """
    chosen: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    used = 0
    for i, options in enumerate(all_options):
        remaining = max_chars - used
        pick = next((o for o in options if len(o[1]) <= remaining), None)
        if pick is None:
            if i > 0:
                break
            pick = options[-1]
        chosen.append(pick)
        used += len(pick[1])
    return chosen
5.5 引用
5.5.1 引用构造
"""引用构造。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from quarry.context.models import Citation
from quarry.retrieval.base import Hit

# 解析档位决定引用能精确到什么程度
_LOCATOR = {"A": "char", "B": "paragraph"}

_DOCS_SQL = """
SELECT id, source_uri, title, parser_tier, meta
FROM documents
WHERE collection_id = $1 AND id = ANY($2::uuid[])
"""

async def fetch_documents(conn, collection_id: UUID,
                          doc_ids: list[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, dict]:
    """批量取文档信息，返回 {文档 ID: 行}。"""
    ids = list(dict.fromkeys(doc_ids))
    if not ids:
        return {}
    rows = await conn.fetch(_DOCS_SQL, collection_id, ids)
    return {r["id"]: dict(r) for r in rows}

def build_citation(hit: Hit, doc: dict | None) -> Citation:
    """文档信息拿不到时也返回一个能用的引用，宁可少几个字段，也不丢掉整条证据。"""
    doc = doc or {}
    meta = doc.get("meta") or {}
    return Citation(
        document_id=hit.document_id,
        source_uri=doc.get("source_uri") or "",
        title=doc.get("title"),
        url=meta.get("url"),
        license=meta.get("license"),
        heading_path=list(hit.heading_path),
        char_start=hit.char_start,
        char_end=hit.char_end,
        locator_kind=_LOCATOR.get(doc.get("parser_tier"), "unknown"),
    )
5.5.2 全量校验引用区间
"""全量校验：A 档文档的每个块，按 [char_start, char_end] 从原文里取出来，必须和 content 一字不差。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path

from quarry.infra.db import acquire, close_pool, init_pool

CORPUS_ROOT = Path("corpus")

async def load_rows():
    await init_pool()
    try:
        async with acquire() as conn:
            return await conn.fetch(
                """SELECT c.chunk_id, c.content, c.char_start, c.char_end,
                          d.source_uri, d.sha256
                   FROM chunks c
                   JOIN documents d ON d.id = c.document_id
                   WHERE d.status = 'ready' AND d.parser_tier = 'A'""")
    finally:
        await close_pool()

def load_source(uri: str, expected_sha: str) -> str | None:
    """读原文。文件不在、或者改过但没重新接入，返回 None。"""
    path = CORPUS_ROOT / uri
    if not path.exists():
        return None
    if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != expected_sha:
        return None
    # 和第三章的解析器保持一致，用 read_text 读（Windows 的换行符会被统一成 \n）
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

def main() -> None:
    rows = asyncio.run(load_rows())
    sources: dict[str, str | None] = {}
    ok, bad, stale = 0, [], set()

    for r in rows:
        uri = r["source_uri"]
        if uri not in sources:
            sources[uri] = load_source(uri, r["sha256"])
        raw = sources[uri]
        if raw is None:
            stale.add(uri)
            continue
        if raw[r["char_start"]:r["char_end"]] == r["content"]:
            ok += 1
        else:
            bad.append(f"  {uri}  [{r['char_start']}, {r['char_end']}]  {r['chunk_id'][:8]}")

    print(f"逐字相符              {ok}")
    print(f"对不上                {len(bad)}")
    print(f"文件改过、未重新接入  {len(stale)} 份")
    for line in bad[:10]:
        print(line)
    if stale:
        print("有文件改过或已不在：先跑一次 ingest_corpus 再校验。")
    if not bad and not stale:
        print("\n✅ 引用区间全部对得上原文")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
